QUESTION: Can the insurer contest this policy after the second insured has died?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING

The query is not asking for a specific policy or person, so it's a general legal question about insurer rights after a second death. Therefore, the only necessary step is to determine the legal rules governing insurer contestability after the death of the second insured, which requires a lookup of insurance law principles related to contestability periods and joint policies.

[death benefit (Payout upon second death)] -> (participates in event: PAID_UPON_SECOND_INSURED_DEATH) -> "Upon the death of the second insured, $2,500,000 will be paid to the designated beneficiary."

[death benefit (Payout upon second death)] -> (participates in event: NOT_PAID_AT_FIRST_DEATH) -> "When the first insured dies, no death benefit is paid."

[death benefit (Payout upon second death)] -> (participates in event: PAID_IMMEDIATELY) -> "If both insureds die in the same accident or incident and it cannot be determined who died first, the death benefit will be paid immediately."

"The policy continues in force covering the surviving insured after the first insured dies." -> RETAINS_COVERED_LIVES -> "This policy insures two lives." -> HAS_PROVISIONS -> "There are no aviation exclusions applicable to this policy."
Logical Conclusions:
The policy continues to insure two lives, even after the first death, maintaining the joint survivorship structure.
The policy insuring two lives includes provisions such as exclusions for suicide and contestability, and rules for simultaneous death.

--- GENERATION ---
LLM answer: No, the insurer cannot contest the policy after the second insured has died because the contestability period for material misrepresentation ends 2 years after the issue date, while both insureds were living.
